# API Compatibility Matrix

Observed: 2026-10-27 (Asia/Bangkok). Scope: repository dependency inventory and deployed Edge runtime comparison; documentation only. Status: BLOCKED. This is static compatibility evidence, not a declaration that flows pass.

## Coverage and evidence

Working-tree HEAD: 48222ffb9c9e60da82d224e1e8aaccd4dd597442. Full request, AGENTS.md, Supabase skill and SUPABASE_MIGRATION_AUDIT.md read. Scanned repository frontend, Edge backend, public assets, migration/import scripts, SQL, reference docs, deployment/build configuration and environment key names. Excluded .git, dependencies/node_modules and lockfiles. Environment values and production user data are never reproduced. AST scan uses installed TypeScript 5.9.3; supplemental line scan covers URLs, SDK/types, config and subscriptions. Array.from is excluded; static method calls are not proof of runtime reachability.

655 repository call records and 205 deployed-source call records appear below. Duplicate records with the same file/line/feature/resource were collapsed. Supplemental references may intentionally overlap call records. Source references are repository-relative; remote/ means /data/supabase/instances/supabase-01/config/volumes/functions/.

Inventory coverage is all statically found matches in scanned files, not 100% behavioral coverage. Dynamic resource arguments, callback routing, variable-assembled fetch URLs, aliases, SQL dynamic execution and external consumers require follow-up. No credentialed end-to-end calls, mutable SQL, real mail, payments, storage operations or migrations executed. No production changes made.

## Compatibility rules

| Code | Contract to preserve | Auth / error / side-effect boundary | Replacement / complexity |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| DATA | supabase-js {data,error,count,status,statusText}; selected projection, nested joins, filters, ordering, pagination; single rejects non-single; maybeSingle permits null | Browser session plus actual DB grants/RLS; public pages may use anon. Service-role backend bypasses RLS and must authorize explicitly. Mutations may fire triggers and alter balances/notifications. Capture PostgREST error code/message/details/hint and callers that ignore error. | Authorized resource API; medium, high for writes/joins/finance |
| RPC | Named arguments and live SQL result type below; SDK wraps result as {data,error}; scalar/object/array semantics differ by function | Existing EXECUTE grants/RLS/SECURITY DEFINER must be verified separately; function existence does not prove authorization. Missing RPC must fail visibly. | Transactional domain API; high |
| EDGE | Caller payload plus exact deployed JSON/status/CORS; SDK {data,error}, including FunctionsHttpError/non-2xx; HTTP 200 can still contain {error} or {success:false} | Per-function auth in EDGE_FUNCTION_MAPPING.md. Do not rely on runtime VERIFY_JWT=false. Mail/payment/admin/file side effects vary by handler. | API, worker, webhook or adapter; high |
| AUTH | GoTrue users/session, access/refresh lifetime, event subscription, persisted local session, OAuth callback, resend/recovery | AuthError checked/thrown by caller; browser getSession is local session lookup, not server authorization. Never expose tokens in reports. Admin methods require server-only privilege. | Auth adapter; high |
| STORAGE | Bucket + object key identity, upload options/upsert, Blob download, publicUrl versus expiring signedUrl | Bucket policies, ownership and public/private visibility; errors are SDK {data,error}; signed URL expiry and origin rewrite matter. Upload/remove modify objects. | Storage adapter; high |
| REALTIME | postgres_changes filters/event payload and channel lifecycle; subscribe returns channel | Session and realtime/RLS configuration must match; reconnect/cleanup and duplicate notifications require tests. No database mutation from subscribe itself. | Authenticated event stream; medium/high |

## Verified missing RPC investigation

Read-only pg_proc / pg_namespace metadata inspected over SSH from the required Docker directory via docker compose exec -T db psql. Current soundpub catalog has 56 functions. Exact-name search across every schema found no entry for the following four RPCs. These remain executable Copyright page callsites; they are not classified as dead code.

| RPC | Caller/context | Live status | Compatibility impact |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| import_composer_royalties | src/pages/CopyrightRoyaltyUpload.tsx:184 handleUpload | ABSENT across all schemas | Flow blocked; PostgREST lookup error expected but not exercised. Confirm intended contract, permissions, data semantics and isolated test before implementing. |
| validate_royalty_csv_row | src/pages/CopyrightRoyaltyUpload.tsx:135 validateCSV | ABSENT across all schemas | Flow blocked; PostgREST lookup error expected but not exercised. Confirm intended contract, permissions, data semantics and isolated test before implementing. |
| delete_royalty_upload | src/pages/CopyrightRoyaltyUploadHistory.tsx:82 handleDelete | ABSENT across all schemas | Flow blocked; PostgREST lookup error expected but not exercised. Confirm intended contract, permissions, data semantics and isolated test before implementing. |
| get_royalty_upload_history | src/pages/CopyrightRoyaltyUploadHistory.tsx:63 loadUploads | ABSENT across all schemas | Flow blocked; PostgREST lookup error expected but not exercised. Confirm intended contract, permissions, data semantics and isolated test before implementing. |

The Edge function delete-royalty-upload exists and uses reverse_royalty_upload; it does not satisfy the absent RPC delete_royalty_upload called by CopyrightRoyaltyUploadHistory. Different API names must not be conflated.

## High-impact behavior and migration blockers

1. Client defaults to schema Soundpub in src/integrations/supabase/client.ts; live function container DATABASE_SCHEMA=soundpub and live DB schema soundpub. VITE_DATABASE_SCHEMA exists in local env files; actual deployed frontend build value was not inspected. Preserve lowercase schema selection deliberately; do not infer frontend settings from function environment.
2. Browser client uses VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY; .env.example instead lists VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY. Build/Docker and client must agree. VITE_ values reach browsers; service-role/provider secrets remain server-side. .env copy was already untracked at baseline and is a source-control exposure blocker owned by coordinator.
3. Auth flows use GoTrue signInWithPassword/signUp, Google signInWithOAuth, resend, resetPasswordForEmail, updateUser and persisted sessions. ForgotPassword/ResetPassword use GoTrue, while legacy custom reset/verification handlers remain deployed. Retain both only with explicit consumer evidence. Remote-only auth-email-hook is enabled in GoTrue and must be included in reproducible runtime source.
4. ICCN/Keycloak sso-login exchanges tokens/codes for Supabase sessions; browser setSession consumes returned credentials. Inspect issuer/audience/subject/linking and callback contracts in isolated tests. Impersonation exposes session credentials to browser state; protect actor/target roles and verify revocation/restoration behavior.
5. Runtime VERIFY_JWT=false. Deployed send-app-email and backup-storage-to-drive have no caller auth; create-user allows admin requests for superadmin. Compatibility must include documented security tightening, with negative role/ownership tests. No security remediation was performed by this documentation-only agent.
6. Preserve exact financial decimal, role-scoped royalty RPC output and idempotency. process-royalty-upload and delete-royalty-upload match deployed bytes despite pre-existing working-tree modifications. Do not replace them with older committed source. Their commit/reverse RPC signatures are live catalog evidence above.
7. Xendit invoice creation returns invoice_url, invoice_id and amount; copyright invoices may include reused. Webhook checks configured callback token, amounts/currency, handles ignored/duplicate statuses and updates payment plus release/registration in separate calls. Retrying after partial success needs validation. Never send test production payments.
8. download-release-invoice requires admin/superadmin and paid invoice, may create PDF/object and update metadata, then returns {invoice_number,url}; signed URL lifetime is 600 s. It is a mutating operation despite download naming.
9. gcs-manage/gcs-upload are disabled but StorageSettings still invokes them. test-gcs still accesses Google APIs. Existing legacy GCS, cloud Supabase and current self-hosted storage URLs require ownership resolution; public URLs and signed URLs are not interchangeable. Storage bucket methods in source must not be mistaken for Data API table access.
10. Realtime listens for profile balance and notification changes. Preserve schema/table filters, user filters, channel unsubscribe and refresh behavior. Subscription existence does not verify publication or live delivery.
11. Public exports/migration-scripts and public OAuth/CORS test assets contain callable integration paths; static inclusion does not establish approved production use. Keep them distinct from application callsites and review shipped assets.

## URL, environment, SDK/type and configuration references

These are dependency references, not independent requests. Repeated SDK import/environment lines are retained for file-level completeness. Documentation/configuration hits are not counted as active callers. Values and raw source lines are omitted to avoid leaking credentials, stored URLs or identity data. Auth, errors and side effects for executable uses inherit their feature contract above; configuration references have no independent response/error or side effect until consumed. Future replacement: runtime configuration, typed API models, storage URL adapter or feature contract named in row; complexity medium/high where a consumer exists, otherwise investigate.

## Required verification before accepting compatibility

- Confirm dynamic resources, variable-assembled fetch URLs, aliases and route reachability; do not use static record counts as behavior coverage percentage.
- Implement or retire the four absent Copyright RPC flows using product evidence; add isolated contract tests for names/arguments/results/errors.
- Validate role and ownership denial/allow matrix for every privileged handler and RPC; preserve DEFAULT DENY.
- Compare PostgREST projections/joins, pagination, nullability, error codes and exact financial values against isolated restore.
- Exercise GoTrue/Google/ICCN callbacks, token refresh/recovery, remote SMTP hook, impersonation restoration and session expiration without production side effects.
- Exercise signed URL expiry/privacy, file upload replacement permissions, invoice generation, webhook idempotency/partial-failure recovery, email delivery and Realtime reconnect in isolation.
- Resolve shared-source/runtime drift and obtain independent QA evidence. This document does not mark Phase 1 or Phase 2 ready.


## Phase 1B addendum — 2026-10-27

All four active Copyright RPCs are classified **BROKEN FEATURE**, not UNKNOWN and not DEAD CODE: `import_composer_royalties`, `validate_royalty_csv_row`, `delete_royalty_upload`, `get_royalty_upload_history`. No replacement was inferred from similarly named distribution-royalty Edge Functions. See `MISSING_RPC_CLOSURE.md`. The features remain unresolved and migration-critical until repaired under approved authorization/accounting contracts or formally retired with caller removal and regression proof.
